Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 20
================================================================================

As a Developer,
I want to upload a fixed OAS file for a specific Session ID,
So that the fixed file can be processed for re-identification.
Scope

- Associate the uploaded file with the provided Session ID.
- Validate the file version before upload.
- Display the upload status and result.
Trigger Action
Developer did case search in the “Anonymized OAS file“ section and case was found as per the Case ID.
Acceptance Criteria
- The “Upload new file version“ section should be present on the Anonymized OAS file page permanently.
- The Upload new file version section shall contain:
  - Case ID search field;
  - File upload area with “Browse files“ functionality;
  - Upload file button. 
- [attachment]
  Developer shall be able to select an .oas file by:
  - Drag and drop;
  - Browse files option.
- If “Upload file“ button was pressed and Case ID is not entered → display the “Case ID“ field in red color and show validation error:
  - Error Message for “Case ID“ field- This field is required;
  - Error Message for “File“ field- This field is required;
    
    [attachment]
- When “Upload file“ button clicked and Case ID and file are provided, the system shall start file upload and display:
  - File name;
  - File size;
  - Uploаd progress bar with persentage;
  - Cancel button.
    [attachment]
- User should be able to cancel file upload in any time during the process via “Cancel“ button.
- As soon, as file selected → should appear next options:
  - Delete selected file - X button;
  - Select other file - “Browse different file“ button.
- Developer shall be able to remove selected file or select another file using Browse different file. 
  [attachment]
- When “Upload file“ button is clicked → system should validate that Case ID is provided and provided one .oas file.
- If there are more than 1 .oas files are uploaded → display error message:
  - Only 1 file can be accepted. 
- When Developer clicks Upload file, the system shall upload the selected file for the provided Case ID.
- The system shall use the provided Case ID to identify the corresponding case and OAS file for which the fixed file is being uploaded.
- When the file is uploaded successfully, the system shall display:
  File uploaded successfully. 
  [attachment]
  
- When the file upload fails, the system shall display:
  - Upload failed. 
  [attachment]
- When the selected OAS file is based on an older version:
- - The file shall be marked as Version conflict detected;
  - The Upload file button shall be disabled;
  - The system shall display:
    File version conflict — This file is based on an older version. Download the latest anonymized OAS file and apply your changes again before uploading.
    [attachment]
    
Testing Notes

- Verify required field validation when Case ID or file is missing.
- Verify successful and failed upload scenarios.
- Verify version conflict handling for an outdated OAS file.

WEB-39265: Formulate baseline KPIs for PHI (patient endpoints)
Status: In Development
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39265
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 20
================================================================================

Define a small set of baseline KPIs for the PHI service's patient personal-data endpoints.
Scope
- Patient personal-data endpoints — patients/bulk, patients/{id}/partial, create/update/patch patients.

Tech notes
Data sources (already available)
- MDC request logs from com.ormco.phiservice.logging.RequestContextMdcFilter — fields endpoint, requestPath, httpStatus, clientApp, durationMs.
- Dashboard/ALB metrics — RequestCount, HTTPCode_Target_2XX/4XX/5XX_Count, TargetResponseTime (p95/p99), ECS CPU/memory, Aurora CPU.

Baseline KPIs
Capture each over a representative window of normal traffic and record the observed value. Values are references, not commitments.
| KPI | Definition | Source | Observed baseline (fill in) |
| Latency p50 / p95 / p99 | durationMs percentiles for patient endpoints (overall + per endpoint) | MDC logs / TargetResponseTime | tbd |
| Server-error rate | 5xx / total requests | MDC httpStatus / ALB 5xx | tbd |
| Client-error rate | 4xx / total requests | MDC httpStatus / ALB 4xx | tbd |
| Success rate | (2xx–3xx) / total requests | MDC httpStatus | tbd |
| Throughput | requests/min for patient endpoints | MDC / ALB RequestCount | tbd |
| Traffic by consumer | request split per clientApp | existing "Requests by clientApp" widget | tbd |

WEB-39475: Test "iTero (iRecord) Direct Transfer" scan submission workflow on prod
Status: Cancelled
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39475
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 18
================================================================================

This workflow spans over many systems. Debugging an issue in prod will be complicated, so it is good to test the end-to-end flow on prod before switching the FF. Here is the full workflow:

- Ingest (scheduled, alignerweb-scans): A cron lambda pulls completed iTero iRecord orders (caseTypeId=13) from the iTero cloud, extracts upper/lower STLs, stores them in S3 and the direct_transfer_scans table, and publishes them via SNS → SQS.
- Doctor selects Direct Transfer (alignerweb-ui): In the Case Wizard "Scans" step the doctor picks "iTero (iRecord) Direct Transfer", which checks their connected iTero accounts and triggers scan matching.
- Match to case (alignerweb-api): The API fuzzy-matches an ingested scan to the case by patient/doctor name (or the doctor manually searches and selects one), records the link in direct_transfer_scan_matches, and queues a copy request to an SQS incoming queue.
- Copy STLs (alignerweb-scans lambda): The copy lambda reads {caseId, orderId, exportDate} and moves the upper/lower STLs into the case — either directly to the API's S3 bucket, or, when FeatureFlags.ScansFromPhi is on, into phi-service via OAuth token → presigned upload URL → S3 PUT → scan-file registration.
- Acknowledge & render (alignerweb-api + UI): The lambda posts SUCCESS/FAILED to an outgoing queue; the API listener generates STL thumbnails, and the UI polls and displays the linked scans.
- Validate at submission (alignerweb-api): On case submission the API enforces that an ITEROSCANS case has scan files, otherwise raises ITERO_SCAN_FILES_ARE_NOT_UPLOADED.

AC
- Perform the full workflow for a test doctor in prod before enabling PHI scans and obtain the logs.
- Enable the PHI scans for the specific test doctor and make sure user technical-scans-admin is being created in prod and 
- Perform the workflow one more time and observe PHI scans usage and make sure no error happens


================================================================================
WEB-39592: Traceability is lost if phi_patient_id is manually changed
Status: Closed
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39592
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 18
================================================================================

Create a patient from within the application. For example the row below gets inserted in patients history table
```
  {
    "id": 181307,
    "phi_patient_id": 90077828,
    "person_birth_date": "2026-09-01",
    "person_first_name": "1",
    "person_gender": "Male",
    "person_last_name": "1",
    "person_middle_name": "",
    "modified_by": "moham-doc-2",
    "modified_at": "2026-09-01 09:50:14",
    "db_session_user": "phi@10.100.13.162",
    "action_type": "INSERT"
  }
```

A db user directly connects to the phi db and update the record above to have different values in all fields including phi_patient_id. For example:
```
  {
    "id": 601421,
    "person_birth_date": "2026-08-01",
    "person_first_name": "2",
    "person_gender": "Female",
    "person_last_name": "2",
    "person_middle_name": "",
    "phi_patient_id": 123,
    "modified_by": null
  }
```

In patients history the record below gets creted:
```
  {
    "id": 181309,
    "phi_patient_id": 123,
    "person_birth_date": "2026-08-01",
    "person_first_name": "2",
    "person_gender": "Female",
    "person_last_name": "2",
    "person_middle_name": "",
    "modified_by": null,
    "modified_at": "2026-09-01 09:57:36",
    "db_session_user": "mpandi@10.100.1.6",
    "action_type": "UPDATE"
  }
```
But there is no way to correlate the UPDATE change above to the original INSERT and thus the history gets lost.

WEB-39172: [Support tool] Admin table
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39172
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 18
================================================================================

It should be possible to view a table of all Admins/Super Admins. The table can be generated on demand. The columns represents department, The rows Countries. The top row, should contain super admins.

User Story
As a Admin/Super Admin tool User
I want to be able to find Admin/Super Admin as per the selected Department / Countries
So that I will know who is the responsible person for specific country/Department 
Scope
- Enable navigation from the Find the Admins tile to the Find the Admins page.
- Add filters with logic:
  - Countries;
  - Department;
- Add Search Results table;
- Add the search error message
Trigger Action
Admin clicks the Find the Admins tile on the Support Admin Toolset Dashboard.
Acceptance Criteria
- The new page “Find the Admins“ should be available:
  - Name - Find the Admins
  - Description - Find Admin by Country and  Department.
- [attachment]On the  “Find the Admins“ page should be present:
  - Country filter;
  - Department filter;
- Country Filter should include:
  - “Select all“ button - on click all the Countries should be selected;
  - If “Select all“ button clicked second time - all the selected Countries should be unselected;
  - List of countries with checkbox for every in the list.
  - The “EU Region“ should be as a drop-down. Default state - collapsed. On click - the list of EU countries should be expanded. 
    [attachment]
  - If “EU Region“ is selected → all the countries included to the EU Region should be selected and vice-versa
    [attachment]
  - If at least 1 country of the list included to the UE region is selected → display “EU region“ with “-“ in the checkbox to highlight this case. 
    [attachment]
  - “Search“ text-field. If at least 1 symbol added to the field → should be applied auto search thru the Countries.  
- Department filter should include:
  [attachment]- “Select all“ button - on click all the Departments should be selected
  - If “Select all“ button clicked second time - all the selected Departments should be unselected;
  - List of Departments with checkbox for every in the list
  - “Search“ text-field. If at least 1 symbol added to the field → should be applied auto search thru the Departments.  
- “Search” button - on click the selected values in the “Countries“ and “Department“ should be applied.
- “Search“ button default state - disabled. Become Enabled, when at least 1 Country or 1 Department is selected.
- When Country and Department are selected → on click Search, the system shall display Admins matching the selected Country/Department combinations.
- When only Country is selected → on click Search, the system shall display Admins for the selected Country across all Departments.
- When only Department is selected → on click Search, the system shall display Admins for the selected Department across all Countries.
- Search Results table should include the filtered data as per the filters applied: 
  - Columns:
    - Country - the name of the selected countries;
    - Department - the name of Department related to the country;
    - Admin - the email/emails of assigned to the Country/Department.
      [attachment]
- Search Results shall include each selected Country/Department combination.
- Admin shall be displayed when both the Country and Department are included in the Admin's responsibility scope.
- If some Country/Department doesn’t have assigned admin → display message in the “Admin“ column 
  - “None“
- In case, if no search results → display error message:
  - No responsible person found. Contact the Super Admin or change the search criteria
    [attachment]

Epic Feature Flag

WEB-39490: Include access_roles and admin_departments in cross-region synced user properties
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39490
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 18
================================================================================

Currently, the access_roles and admin_departments columns are not included in the cross-region-synced properties of a user. If an admin/super admin opens a user details page in a non-home region (as part of this ticket), they will not have the required access to update the target user. 

AC
- updates in access_roles and admin_departments should propagate across regions.

WEB-39195: patients_history.modified_by reflects last app user, not actual DB editor, on direct DB UPDATE/DELETE
Status: Closed
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39195
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 18
================================================================================

When we have patient created by a user (for example 'moham-doc-2') from the UI we will see the following in patients_history.

```
"id": 8,
"phi_patient_id": 7262022,
"person_birth_date": "2026-08-01",
"person_first_name": "John",
"person_gender": "Male",
"person_last_name": "Smith",
"person_middle_name": "",
"modified_by": "moham-doc-2",
"modified_at": "2026-08-12 08:59:41",
"db_session_user": "phi@192.168.65.1",
"action_type": "INSERT"
```
Then if someone edits the same patient directly from DB user (change first name "John" -> "Peter") we get the following
```
"id": 9,
"phi_patient_id": 7262022,
"person_birth_date": "2026-08-01",
"person_first_name": "Peter",
"person_gender": "Male",
"person_last_name": "Smith",
"person_middle_name": "",
"modified_by": "moham-doc-2",
"modified_at": "2026-08-12 09:00:54",
"db_session_user": "root@192.168.65.1",
"action_type": "UPDATE"
```
Modified by is still "moham-doc-2" which is incorrect. 
We have this issue also for "action_type": "DELETE".

WEB-39295: [Support Tool] [Super Admin] Expand Admin row to view responsible Users
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39295
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 17; S&C Sprint 2026 18
================================================================================

As an Super Admin,
I want to expand an Admin row on the Responsible Users page,
So that  I can view non-Admin Users for whom the selected Admin is responsible within my department.
Scope
- Add expandable Admin rows to the Responsible Users table for Super Admins
Trigger Action
- Super Admin clicks an Admin row on the Responsible Users page.
Acceptance Criteria
- When a Super Admin clicks an Admin row, the row shall expand and display non-Admin Users for whom the selected Admin is responsible of within the Super Admins department/s.
- The expanded list shall display only non-Admin Users who are within both:
  - the selected Admin's responsibility scope;
  - the requesting Super Admin's department/s.
- A non-Admin User shall be considered within the selected Admin's responsibility scope when:
  - the User shares at least one Department and one Country with the Admin
- A non-Admin User shall be considered within the requesting Super Admin's responsibility scope when the User’s Department matches at least one Super Admin’s department.
- When the Super Admin clicks the expanded Admin row again, the list of non-Admin Users shall be collapsed.
- When no non-Admin Users meet both the Admin and Super Admin responsibility rules, the expanded row shall display no User records and the following message shall be shown: No Users found.
[attachment]Testing Notes
Scenario 1 — User belongs to both responsibility scopes
Super Admin Departments: R_AND_D, SALES
Admin Departments: R_AND_D, OPERATIONS
Admin Countries: US
User Department: R_AND_D
User Country: US
User is displayed under the expanded Admin.
Scenario 2 — User belongs to Admin scope but not Super Admin scope
Super Admin Departments: R_AND_D, SALES
Admin Departments: R_AND_D, OPERATIONS
Admin Countries: US
User Department: OPERATIONS
User Country: US
User is not displayed under the expanded Admin.

WEB-37657: [Support Tool] Notify Super Admins when no Admin is assigned to Department or Country
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-37657
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 17
================================================================================

As a department Super Admin,
I want to receive a notification when no Admin is assigned to a specific country within a department,
So that all operational areas always have responsible Admin.
Scope
- Implement automatic validation to detect missing Admin coverage within Departments and notify the responsible Department Super Admin(s).
- The system shall validate Admin coverage for every Country within each Department.
- If one or more Countries within a Department do not have an assigned Admin, a notification shall be sent.
- If Country doesn’t have an assigned Admin and doesn’t have an assigned non-Admin User, so no notification message should be sent.
- If all Countries within a Department have Admin coverage, no notification shall be sent.
Reasons of missing Admin:
- One of the following events occurs:
  - ADMIN role removed from a user
  - Department removed from Admin responsibility list
  - Country removed from Admin responsibility list
  - Admin user deactivated (ST → User Details page → Deactivate/Reactivate button)
  - A new Country is added to a Department without assigning an Admin.
Acceptance criteria
- The notification shall be sent to the Super Admin(s) responsible for the Department if there is at least one Country within that Department that does not have an active Admin assigned.
- One email shall be generated per affected Department once per day after the scheduled validation.
- If a Super Admin is responsible for multiple affected Departments, a separate notification email shall be generated for each affected Department.
- The notification email subject shall be:
  [Support Tool] No Admin Assigned in <Department> Warning
- The notification email shall contain (Email draft): 
Hello,
No Admin is currently assigned to the following operational area:
Department: <department>
<AND>
Country: <country/ies> - <list of countries separated by commas>
This may impact user management and operational support workflows in Support Tool.
Please review Admin responsibility assignments and assign a responsible Admin for this area.
Open Support Tool:
<link to Support Tool home page>
Thank you,
Support Tool Notifications

Mock-ups: Link to Figma
Testing Notes
- A Country within a Department shall be considered covered when at least one active Admin is assigned to the same Department and Country.
Feature Flag
```
Epic Feature Flag = Department_Based_User_Management
```
Notes:

Translations
| English (EN) | German (DE) | Spanish (ES) | French (FR) | Italian (IT) | Japanese (JP) | Portuguese (PT) | Russian (RU) | Chinese (CN) |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

WEB-39186: [Admin] Restrict Cancel Case button for admins without Support role
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39186
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 17
================================================================================

As an Admin without Support role,
I want access to the Cancel Case button to depend on Support role
So that only Admins with Support role can cancel the case.
Trigger Action
- Admin without Support role opens a case details.
Scope
Applies to:
- Cancel Case button
Acceptance criteria
- GIVEN Admin without Support role
  WHEN Admin opens a case details
  THEN Cancel Case button is disabled
  AND WHEN Admin hovers over the Cancel Case
  THEN a tooltip is displayed with the message: Access denied. You don’t have permission to update a case.

WEB-39228: [Support Tool] Notify Users without assigned role or department before deactivation
Status: Cancelled
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-39228
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 17
================================================================================

User Story
As a DTX User without an assigned role or department,
I want to be notified before my account is automatically deactivated,
So that I have sufficient time to contact my DTX Admin and request the required assignment.
Scope
Identify active DTX Users who have no role or department assigned.
Notify affected Users before the scheduled deactivation date.
Preconditions
- The User account is active.
- The User has no assigned role or no assigned department.
- The automatic account deactivation functionality is enabled.
Trigger Action
The scheduled account validation process checks active DTX Users for missing role or department assignments.
Acceptance Criteria
- The system shall identify active Users who have:
  - no role assigned; OR
  - no department assigned.
- A User identified as having no assigned role or department shall be scheduled for deactivation 60 days after the feature release go-live date.
- The system shall send a notification to the affected User 60 days before the scheduled deactivation date with the following message:
  - “Your DTX account currently has no role or department assigned and is scheduled for deactivation in 60 days. If this is not expected, please contact your DTX Admin to request the appropriate role and department assignments before the deactivation date.“
- If the User still has no role or department assigned, the system shall send another notification 10 days before the scheduled deactivation date with the following message:
  - “Your DTX account currently has no role or department assigned and is scheduled for deactivation in 10 days. If this is not expected, please contact your DTX Admin to request the appropriate role and department assignments before the deactivation date.“
- If the User still has no role or department assigned, the system shall send another notification 5 days before the scheduled deactivation date with the corresponding 5 days wording.
  - “Your DTX account currently has no role or department assigned and is scheduled for deactivation in 5 days. If this is not expected, please contact your DTX Admin to request the appropriate role and department assignments before the deactivation date.“
- If the User still has no role or department assigned, the system shall send another notification 1 day before the scheduled deactivation date with the corresponding 1 day wording.
  - “Your DTX account currently has no role or department assigned and is scheduled for deactivation in 1 day. If this is not expected, please contact your DTX Admin to request the appropriate role and department assignments before the deactivation date.“
- If the required role and department are assigned to the User before the scheduled deactivation date:
  - the User shall no longer be eligible for automatic deactivation;
  - no further deactivation notifications shall be sent to the User;
  - the User account shall remain active.
Feature Flag
Epic Feature Flag
Testing Notes
Special attention should be given to legacy Users where access_roles = NULL.
access_roles = NULL shall be treated as no assigned role for the purpose of notification and automatic deactivation.
Until such a User is deactivated, access_roles = NULL shall not by itself exclude the User from Responsibility Scope if the User otherwise satisfies the applicable Department/Country responsibility rules.


================================================================================
WEB-37656: [Support Tool] Notify Super Admins when no Super Admin is assigned to a Department
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-37656
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 17
================================================================================

As a Super Admin,
I want to receive a notification when a department has no assigned Super Admin,
So that all departments always have responsible Super Admin.
Scope
- Implement automatic validation to detect missing Super Admin coverage for Departments and notify Super Admins.
- The system shall validate Super Admin coverage for every predefined Departments.
- The validation shall consider only active Super Admin users assigned to one or more Departments.
- If one or more Departments do not have an assigned Super Admin, a notification shall be sent.
- If all Departments have Super Admin coverage, no notification shall be sent.
Reasons of missing Super Admin
- One of the following events occurs:
  - SUPER_ADMIN role removed from a user
  - Super Admin user deactivated (Support Tool → User details page → Deactivate/Reactivate button)
  - All Departments are removed from a Super Admin.
  - A new Department is introduced without assigning any Super Admin.
Acceptance criteria
- The notification shall be sent to all active Super Admins if one or more Departments do not have a Super Admin assigned.
- One email shall be generated and sent to all active Super Admins once per day after the scheduled validation.
- If multiple Departments do not have an assigned Super Admin, a single notification email shall include all uncovered Departments.
- The email trigger time should be the same as for other jobs (no specific time required).
- The notification email subject shall be:
  [Support Tool] No Super Admin Assigned Warning
Email Sample:
Hello,
No Super Admin is currently assigned to the following department:
Department: <department>
This may impact permission approval workflows and notification routing in Support Tool.
Please review Super Admin responsibility assignments and assign a responsible Super Admin for this department.
Open Support Tool:
<link to Support Tool home page>
Thank you,
Support Tool Notifications

Feature Flag: Epic feature flag
```
Department_Based_User_Management
```

WEB-38465: [Support Tool] [Admin/Super Admin] Search Responsible Users
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-38465
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 16; S&C Sprint 2026 17
================================================================================

As an Admin/Super Admin,
I want to search for Users within my responsibility scope,
So that I can quickly find a specific User.
Scope
- Add a search field to the Responsible Users page.
Acceptance Criteria
- The Responsible Users page shall display a search field above the table.
- The search functionality should be applied to the data in the table. 
- The search field shall display the placeholder "Search by User Name".
  - the placeholder should disappear when the user enters the field
  - The placeholder shall be displayed again when the search field is empty and loses focus.
- The search action shall be initiated by pressing Enter on the keyboard or by clicking the Search button in the UI.
  [attachment]
- When the user performs a search, the table shall display only Users whose User Name contains the entered value within the requester's responsibility scope.
- The search shall be case-insensitive.
- When the search field is cleared, the table shall display all Users within the requester's responsibility scope.
- The current sorting order shall be preserved while search results are displayed.
- When no Users match the search criteria, the table shall display no records and the message  "No Users found."

Mock-ups: Link to Figma

- Admin without Support role is impersonating a Doctor and opens an unsubmitted case → Prescription tab.

  AND Admin is impersonating a Doctor

  AND Prescription was updated during case creation
  THEN Edit Prescription button is disabled
  AND WHEN Admin hovers over the Edit Prescription
  THEN a tooltip is displayed with the message: Access denied. You don’t have permission to update a case.

WEB-36526: Structured Logging with Logback & MDC
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-36526
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 12
================================================================================

Replace plain text logging with structured JSON logging that includes request context in every log line.
ACs
- When the application processes any request, the log output is in JSON format (not plain text).
- Every log line produced during a request includes the HTTP method, the request path, all path variables, and all query/request parameters.
- Existing API endpoints return the same responses as before (nothing changes for the end user).
- The JSON  properties should be parsed into a discoverable field at ingestion, so in Logs Insights we should be able to filter and aggregate directly on those fields. For example:
  ```
  fields httpMethod, endpoint, httpStatus, durationMs
  | filter httpStatus >= 500
  ```
Technical Notes
- Logs emitted in structured JSON format via Logback (e.g., logstash-logback-encoder).
- A servlet filter populates MDC with httpMethod and requestPath per request.
- Interceptor preHandle: iterate HandlerMapping.URI_TEMPLATE_VARIABLES_ATTRIBUTE and request.getParameterMap(), add each key/value to MDC with a prefix (e.g., pathVar.caseId, param.scanId) to avoid collisions.


================================================================================
WEB-37477: Integrate Support Tool <-> PHI photos
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-37477
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 12
================================================================================

AC
Cover the following APIs:
| Endpoint | Use case |
| GET /support/cases/{caseId}/photos  | Cases page → search/select a case → the Photos row auto-loads thumbnails. |
| POST /support/cases/{caseId}/photos/{position}  | Cases page → Photos row → click Upload on the desired photo slot (e.g. EXTRAORAL_FRONTAL_SMILING) → pick a file. |
| DELETE /support/cases/{caseId}/photos/{position}  | Cases page → Photos row → click Delete on a photo card → confirm. |
| POST /support/cases/{caseId}/photos/repairs  | Cases page → Photos row → when the "non-JPEG detected" warning banner appears, click Fix this. |

WEB-36781: Benchmark bulk vs. single case access check in alignerweb-api to evaluate optimization need
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-36781
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 09
================================================================================

Endpoint GET /bulk is protected with @PreAuthorize("@caseAccessValidator.hasAccess(#phiPatientIds)"). This protection calls GET /api/v1/cases/phiPatientIds/accessToAll?phiPatientIds=... on alignerweb-api, the implementation does the following for every ID in the list:
- Fetches all matching CaseEntity records from the DB replica in a single query (findByPhiPatientIdInAndCancelledFalse)
- Iterates over each result and calls permissionCheckerService.checkCurrentUserAccess(...) individually
The open question is: does checkCurrentUserAccessToAllCases scale acceptably when called with N IDs vs. 1 ID? 

Goal: Determine whether the bulk check implementation is efficient enough or needs optimization before it becomes a bottleneck under production load.
AC:
- Using CloudWatch or application logs, determine the current request frequency of doctor home page in production (requests/min at p50 and peak) to use as the baseline concurrency target for load tests
- Benchmark GET /api/v1/cases/phiPatientIds/accessToAll on alignerweb-api with a bulk of IDs
- Profile CaseServiceImpl.checkCurrentUserAccessToAllCases: measure DB query time separately from the per-entity checkCurrentUserAccess loop
- Compare latency of the single-ID path (wrapped in a list) vs. a hypothetical dedicated single-check path
- Monitor Aurora read replica CPUUtilization during load tests with 1 vs. N IDs to determine whether the findByPhiPatientIdInAndCancelledFalse query causes disproportionate CPU growth as the IN clause grows, and flag if vertical scaling is needed
- Document results and recommend whether the bulk logic overhead is acceptable or needs optimization

WEB-36898: Create a CloudWatch operational dashboard for the PHI service
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-36898
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 08
================================================================================

Add an Dashboard resource to template.yaml that provides operational visibility into the PHI service. The dashboard is fully parameterized and recreated on each deployment in the corresponding environment.
ACs
- A new AWS::CloudWatch::Dashboard CloudFormation resource exists in template.yaml, named after the stack (${AWS::StackName}). It is visible in the AWS CloudWatch console under Dashboards.
- The dashboard contains the following widgets:
  - Quick links — clickable links to the ECS Service, CloudWatch Log Group, and ALB Target Group in the AWS console.
  - PHI requests per minute — total ALB request count (Sum, 1 min).
  - PHI latency (p95) — target response time at p95 (1 min).
  - PHI target status codes — request counts grouped by HTTP status: 2xx, 4xx, 5xx.
  - PHI target health — healthy vs unhealthy host count.
  - PHI tasks: desired vs running — ECS Container Insights task counts.
  - PHI ECS CPU utilization — average CPU usage.
  - PHI ECS memory utilization — average memory usage.
  - Shared ALB 5xx (context) — ELB-level 5xx count for the shared load balancer.
  - PHI log errors — CloudWatch Logs Insights query showing error count over time (5 min bins), filtering for ERROR|Exception.
  - PHI top errors — CloudWatch Logs Insights table of the top 10 most frequent error messages.

WEB-35796: [Admin] Save/replace/delete DICOM files via PHI Data Service during Doctor impersonation (/caseWizard)
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-35796
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 05; S&C Sprint 2026 06
================================================================================

Scope
- Covers the CBCT - DICOM Submission section on the Scans step (/caseWizard).
Acceptance criteria
- When Admin impersonates a Doctor and uploads a DICOM file on the Scans step (/caseWizard) and clicks Submit, DTX sends the file to PHI Data Service, if the Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission.
  - If Admin doesn’t have DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE, Admin should see an error message:
    ”Access denied. You don’t have permission to upload scan files.”
- When Admin impersonates a Doctor and replaces a DICOM file by clicking Update → Submit a new file, DTX updates the file in PHI Data Service, if the Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission.
  - If Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY or DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY or no permission, Admin should see an error message: ”Access denied. You don’t have permission to upload scan files.”
- When Admin impersonates a Doctor and deletes a DICOM file by clicking Remove DICOM, DTX updates the file in PHI Data Service, if the Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission.
  - If Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY or DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY or no permission, Admin should see an error message: ”Access denied. You don’t have permission to delete scan files.”

WEB-35798: [Admin] Save/retrieve iTero (without subscription) scans via PHI Data Service during Doctor impersonation (/caseWizard)
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-35798
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 06
================================================================================

Scope
- Covers the iTero (without subscription) option on the Scans step (/caseWizard)
Acceptance Criteria
- When Admin impersonates a Doctor and uploads any iTero scan file (upper, lower, or combined) on the Scans step (/caseWizard), DTX sends the file to PHI Data Service, if the Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission.
  - If Admin doesn’t have DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE, Admin should see an error message:
    ”Access denied. You don’t have permission to upload scan files.”
- When Admin impersonates a Doctor and previews the uploaded iTero scan on /caseWizard, DTX retrieves the file from PHI Data Service, if the Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ or DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission.
  - If Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY or no permission, Admin should see an error message:
    ”Access denied. You don’t have permission to view scans.”
- When Admin impersonates a Doctor and deletes or replaces an iTero scan file, DTX removes the file from PHI Data Service, if the Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission.
  - If Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY or DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY or no permission, Admin should see an error message:
    ”Access denied. You don’t have permission to delete scan files.”
- Should be controlled under the feature flag.

Technical notes:
- FE
  - error messages handling

WEB-35810: Transfer Cases page: Admin ignores PHI permissions for doctors with >800 cases
Status: Closed
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-35810
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 04; S&C Sprint 2026 05
================================================================================

Environment/Version: Release QA
Precondition: Admin is managing doctor with more than 800 cases. Set admin’s PHI data access permission to None.
STR:
- Login to support tool as admin in phase Dual Write, Fallback Read from DTX or admin in phase Dual Write, Read only from phi-service
- Navigate to Transfer cases page and List cases from doctor
- request is sent to: ‘phi-service/api/v1/patients/bulk?fields=phiPatientId,personFirstName,personLastName,personBirthDate&phiPatientIds=’
  with all cases listed in query
Actual:
- If admin is in Dual Write, Fallback Read from DTX: call to this endpoint retrieves status 400, but cases are retrieved without respecting phi access permission, all PHI data is shown even if admin does not have correct PHI access permission. Status 400 is returned only for requests where the query exceeds ~6000 characters. If the same request is shortened to below ~6000 characters, the response status is 200 and the request is processed successfully
- If admin is in Dual Write, Read only from phi-service: call to this endpoint retrieves status 400, and error message is shown: Something went wrong while fetching patient data
Expected: Regardless of the number of characters in the query, an admin without the corresponding PHI access permissions must not be able to view the data

Image with same request with shortened query (less cases) is IN ATTACHEMTNS

Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-34408
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 03
================================================================================

Scope
Supports upload, preview, delete, and replace based on Admin’s PHI permissions.
Acceptance Criteria
- When impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission and uploads the scans, STL files saved to PHI Data Service.
  - if Admin doesn’t have PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission and uploads the scans, STL files are not uploaded
    - Admin sees a message: “Access Denied: You don't have permission to upload patient data.“
- When impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY and previews the STL on /caseWizard page, DTX retrieves it from PHI Data Service.

    - Admin sees a message: “Access Denied: You don’t have permission to view scans.“
- When impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE and deletes/replaces a scan(s) on /caseWizard page, DTX removes the PHI file from the PHI data service.
  - if Admin doesn’t have PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission and deletes/replaces a scan(s) on /caseWizard page, scans are not removed from PHI data service.
    - Admin sees a message: “Access denied: you don’t have permission to delete patient data.“
- Must be controlled by the Feature Flag.

WEB-34985: Patient page - Notes (PHI)
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-34985
Mentioned in: S&C Sprint 2026 03
================================================================================

When doctor/staff adds note on a Patient page and patient account is transferred to PHI data service
Then data shall be stored in PHI data service
So That process of storing sensitive information of notes would be compliant
Acceptance criteria:
- When user adds a new note from Patient page, then information about the notes shall be stored in PHI data service
- When user adds a new note from Patient page and patient record does not exist in PHI data service, then information about the notes shall be stored in DTX database
- When user enters a Patient page, information about the notes shall be retrieved from PHI data service
- When user enters a Patient page and patient record does not exist in PHI data service, information about the notes shall be retrieved from DTX database
- When user adds a new note from Patient page, patient record exists in PHI data service and system error occurs, note shall not be saved in PHI and DTX database
Feature Flag and location (desktop/web):
```
Feature Flag name
```
Notes:
- UX is implemented within https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-33377 
- Related implementation from Phase 1:
  - https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-34492 
  - https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-34681 
- Logic of sending and retrieving the data shall depend on existing feature flags of record migrations:
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/SC/pages/5774639119/Users+migration+phases+v2
  - If status is NONE, data shall be received from DTX
  - If status is not NONE, , data shall be received from PHI Data Service
Technical details
WBS
- BE
  - Introduce Notes table in PHI DB
  - API in PHI Service for the table
  - Remove the same table from Web DTX DB
  - Remove the API for the table in Web DTX DB
  - NB: phi_patient_id is already available
- FE
  - Switch from calling Web DTX API to call PHI Service API
- QA
  - High Medium
  - AQA
    - n/a

As an Admin
I want to enable forwarding only for a specific case that is restricted by a Forwarding disabled Doctor alert,
So that the assigned Operator can forward this case.
Mock-ups: https://www.figma.com/design/dRhpmQCrmnhtcyj2MA0cWi/CMS?node-id=573-22489&t=uMW5GXymwzW91AXh-0 
Scope
- Add a case-level action on the Case details page that allows Admins to remove the forwarding restriction inherited from the doctor for this case only.
- This action does not modify or remove the doctor’s alert configuration globally.
UI / UX
- Add an ENABLE FORWARDING button at the top of the Case Details page (same row as HOLD, PRIORITIZE, RE-ASSIGN, CANCEL).
  
  [attachment]
Acceptance Criteria
- Admins see the ENABLE FORWARDING button when:
  - The case belongs to a doctor with a Forwarding disabled Doctor alert AND
  - The case is in a step where forwarding is allowed:
    - Anatomy
    - Design
    - DesignQC
      AND
  - The case is assigned to operator AND
  - Forwarding has not yet been enabled for this specific case.
- If Doctor Alert has been removed from a Doctor associated with the case OR the Forward Allowed is set to YES in Doctor Alert configuration applied to the Doctor associated with the case:
  - ENABLE FORWARDING buttons should be removed from the Design case details page.
- ENABLE FORWARDINGonly visible in admin view.
- When Admin clicks ENABLE FORWARDING, a confirmation modal appears:
  - Title: "Enable Forwarding for This Case"
  - Message: “This case is restricted by a forwarding disabled doctor alert. Enabling forwarding will allow the assigned operator to forward this case once. The doctor’s restriction will then be restored automatically. Other cases remain unaffected. Do you want to proceed?”
  - Buttons:
    - Yes
    - Cancel
- If Admin clicks Cancel, the modal closes and the case remains restricted.
- If Admin clicks Yes:
  - The case no longer inherits the Forwarding disabled Doctor alert
  - The doctor-level alert remains unchanged
  - The assigned Operator now sees the Forward button enabled and can forward the case.
  - ENABLE FORWARDING button is hidden.
  - DISABLE FORWARDING button is displayed.
  - Order Note entry is created:
    "Doctor alert forwarding restriction removed for this case by <admin_user_name>."
- Once the Operator forwards the case:
  - The forwarding override automatically expires.
  - The case again fully inherits all Forwarding disabled Doctor Alert.
  - Forward button becomes disabled for the next Operator assigned to this case (unless Admin enables forwarding again).
  - ENABLE FORWARDING button becomes visible again (if all conditions in AC1 are met).
- If the case is affected by any other system or case alert that prevents forwarding, the Operator must still be unable to forward the case, even if the Admin has enabled forwarding for this specific case caused by the Doctor Alert
- When forwarding has been enabled for a specific case and the case is re-assigned from one Operator to another:
  - The case-level forwarding enabled override becomes inactive for this case.
- When forwarding has been enabled for a case and the case is rolled back to an earlier step:
  - The case-level forwarding enabled override becomes inactive for this case.
- If the Operator assigned to the case becomes inactive OR auto-assign is disabled AND case moves one CMS status back (forward <- accept):
  - The case-level forwarding enabled override becomes inactive for this case.
Feature Flag:
```
Epic Feature Flag
```

Notes
- If a case is affected by two or more Doctor alerts and one or more of these alerts has forwarding disabled, THEN:
  - Clicking ENABLE FORWARDING creates a single case-level override that bypasses the forwarding restrictions from all Doctor alerts for this specific case.
Testing Notes:
- Other cases for the same doctor continue to inherit the Forwarding disabled alert normally.
- Only this specific case receives the override.
- The case can only be overridden once. Any subsequent changes in status (including rollback, canceling/uncanceling, etc.), as well as any changes to assigned accounts, will cause it to lose its privilege.
Technical Notes:
- CMS
  - FE:
    - to add button visible by ForwardAllowed 
    - to add modal window
  - BE:
    - CaseAlertAssignment table
      - to add new field singleForwardAllowed
    - new endpoint to set singleForwardAllowed for specific case Doctor Alert Assignment
      - @PatchMapping("{orderId}/activeCaseAlertAssignments")
      - to add order log
    - to update /activeCaseAlertAssignments to override isForwardAllowed by singleForwardAllowedfor specific alert assignment
    - to  update /processStep/approval 
      - to override isForwardAllowed by singleForwardAllowedfor specific alert assignment while check isCaseForwardForbiddenByAssignedCaseAlerts
      - to drop singleForwardAllowed to FALSE for all active CaseAlertAssignments

WEB-33220: [Admin] [JSP] Doctor Impersonation: Retrieve Photos on /redirectFromSave and /editPhotos
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-33220
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 25
================================================================================

When an Admin impersonates a Doctor and opens the Photos tab via /redirectFromSave or /editPhotos, DTX must retrieve patient photos from PHI Data Service instead of DTX storage.
The PHI Data Service evaluates the impersonating Admin’s PHI permission level and returns photos only if the Admin has the appropriate access.
Scope
- Replace the current photo retrieval on /redirectFromSave and /editPhotos with a call to PHI Data Service, using the Admin’s PHI permissions for validation.
Acceptance Criteria
- When the impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY or → photos are displayed in the Photos tab.
- When the impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY or no PHI permission → placeholders are displayed instead of photos.
- Photos are loaded from PHI Data Service for both /redirectFromSave and /editPhotos pages.
Technical Notes:
- alignerweb
  - FE: update page to call phi-service under the FF
    - to add attributes
    - to call existing phi-service logic

Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-33219
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 25
================================================================================

When an Admin impersonates a Doctor and opens a case in DTX (/getPatientDetails), patient photos in the Patient Details and Photos/Scans tabs (including enlarged photos) must be retrieved from the PHI Data Service.
The PHI Data Service evaluates the impersonating Admin’s PHI permission level and returns photos only if the Admin has the appropriate access.
Scope
- Replace the current /getPatientDetails photo retrieval with a call routed through PHI Data Service, using the Admin’s PHI permission for validation.
Acceptance Criteria
- When the impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY → photos are displayed in the Patient Details and Photos/Scans tabs, including enlarged photos.
- When the impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY or no PHI permission → photos are not displayed; placeholders appear instead.
- When the impersonating Admin has PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY or no PHI permission → enlarged photos are not displayed; placeholders appear instead.
Technical Notes:
- BE alignerweb-api
  - new endpoint for getting thumbnails
- BE phi-service 
  - to proxy new endpoint in DTX
or we can use /{caseId}/photos-detail
- FE alignerweb
  - to call new phi-service endpoint

CMS-11255: [Admin] Assign Operator to Consultant Queue
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-11255
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 25
================================================================================

As an Admin,
I want to assign an Design/Anatomy Operator to a Consultant Queue,
So that this Operator becomes available as the designated Consultant for that queue.
Mock-ups: https://www.figma.com/design/dRhpmQCrmnhtcyj2MA0cWi/CMS?node-id=26826-196879&t=N0yzoyzZWl6463eP-4 
Scope
- Extend CMS → Supports → Consultant Support page with the ability to assign Operators to queues.
- The assignment workflow should mirror the way Admins assign Operators to Operator Pools.
- Each Operator can only be linked to one Consultant Queue (no duplicate assignments allowed).
- 
Acceptance criteria:
- Design Operators can only be assigned to the following Consultant Queues:
  - Design CPL
  - Design Lead
- Anatomy Operators can only be assigned to the following Consultant Queues:
  - Anatomy CPL
  - Anatomy Lead
  - Anatomy Rework
- When an Admin navigates to Supports → Consultant Support → selects:
  - Design CPL
  - Design Lead
  - Anatomy CPL
  - Anatomy Lead
  - Anatomy Rework
    -> Assigned button from Assigned Accounts section becomes active.
    
    [attachment]
- If Admin clicks the Assign button, the dialog window with the following content appears:
  - Title: ASSIGN CONSULTANT TO <queue_name>
  - Insert Assign button - becomes active if at least one user selected
  - Cancel button - discards changes and closes dialog window
  - Consultant queue <queue_name>
  - Process <process_name>:
    - For Design CPL/Design Lead queues the process step should be Design with the list of Design Operators available for selection
    - For Anatomy CPL/Anatomy Lead/Anatomy Rework queues the process step should be Anatomy with the list of Anatomy Operators available for selection.
  - Table with the list of users available for assignment with columns:
    - UserID with checkbox - multiselection is possible
    - UseName
      
      [attachment]
- The Admin can select a user by checking the box and confirm the assignment by clicking the Insert Assign button.
- Once assigned, the user immediately appears in the Assigned Operators panel as the designated Consultant for that queue.
  
  [attachment]
- When a new Consultant is added to a queue, they shall appear at the top of the list of assigned Consultants.
- Operator can’t be assigned to the same queue, if they are already assigned:
  - the operator should not be available for selection.
Feature Flag and location (desktop/web):
```
Feature Flag name
```
Notes:
- Operators assigned to a queue will be treated as Consultant accounts within that queue.
- It is possible to assign Operator to multiple queues at the same process step
Technical Note:
cms-ui
- to add existing component from operator pool

WEB-32247: [Super Admin/Admin] Introduce Role Management on Manage Users page
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-32247
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 24
================================================================================

As an Super Admin/Admin,
I want to manage user roles via a new User Role section on the Manage Users page in Support Tool,
So that assigning predefined roles configures the appropriate access across Spark applications.
Scope:
- Introduce a new User Roles section between Feature Permissions and Access Permissions on the Manage Users page.
Acceptance criteria:
- A new User Role section is visible between Feature Permissions and Access Permissions on the Manage Users page for Admin users.
- The section displays a list of checkboxes for all predefined roles:
  - Super Admin with the following set of permissions:
    - DTX_ACCESS
    - CMS_ACCESS
    - SUPPORT_TOOL_ACCESS
    - SUPPORT_TOOL_CREATE_ADMIN
    - SUPPORT_TOOL_EDIT_PERMISSIONS
  - Admin:
    - DTX_ACCESS
    - CMS_ACCESS
    - SUPPORT_TOOL_ACCESS
    - SUPPORT_TOOL_EDIT_PERMISSIONS
  - Sales:
    - DTX_ACCESS
    - PHI_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY
  - Support:
    - DTX_ACCESS
    - CMS_ACCESS
    - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE
    - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ
    - SUPPORT_TOOL_ACCESS
  - Developer:
    - DTX_ACCESS
    - CMS_ACCESS
  - CMS Supervisor:
    - DTX_ACCESS
    - CMS_ACCESS
    - SUPPORT_TOOL_ACCESS
    - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE
    - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ
  - Default:
    - DTX_ACCESS
- An Update Role button is available within the User Role section.
- Update Role button is disabled until no changes made.
- When the Update Role button is clicked:
  - The selected roles are applied.
  - Corresponding Access Permissions checkboxes are updated to reflect the selected role(s).
- When one or more roles are deselected and Update Role is clicked:
  - The permissions associated with deselected roles are removed unless still included in another selected role.
- The user can select multiple roles at once. Resulting permissions are a union of all selected role permissions.
- An info tooltip is added next to the section title with the text:
  “Selecting a role automatically configures access permissions across Spark applications.”
- For new users, the User Role section appears with Default role selected by default.
Mock-ups: Link to Figma
Feature Flag and location (desktop/web):
```
Feature Flag name
```
Notes:

Technical Notes:
- alignerweb
  - to create new column access_roles  in users table
- alignerweb-sso
  - to update access_roles with Default value for the newly created user
- alignerweb-support
  - FE
    - to create dropdown to show and choose roles for admins (uses new PUT endpoint)
  - BE
    - to create an PUT endpoint to grant corresponding permissions to the user ( to handle intersections)
    - to update some endpoint to provide to UI applied access_roles

WEB-32846: [Admin] Retrieve PHI data on Print Prescription forms during Operator impersonation
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-32846
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 24
================================================================================

Scope
- Pages in scope:
  - /printCaseSubmissionForm – Primary Spark Retainer cases and Primary Aligner cases.
  - /secondaryPrintCaseSubmissionForm – Refinement cases.
  - /printReorderSubmissionForm – Reorder cases (available for Manufacturing and Shipping Operators).
- Roles covered under impersonation: Anatomy Operator, Design Operator, Design QC, Manufacturing Operator, Shipping Operator.
Acceptance
- When an Admin impersonating an Operator navigates to a Case Details page → Prescription tab → clicks the Print Prescription button, DTX calls the PHI Data Service to retrieve first_name, last_name, gender, and birth_date.
- Data must populate both the print window and the corresponding web page
  - (/printCaseSubmissionForm
  - /secondaryPrintCaseSubmissionForm
  - /printReorderSubmissionForm).
- If the impersonating Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY permission, real values for all four PHI fields are retrieved and displayed.
- If the impersonating Admin has DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY permission:
  - first_name, last_name → real values
  - gender, birth_date → masked as *****
- If the impersonating Admin has no PHI permission, all four PHI fields (first_name, last_name, gender, birth_date) are masked as *****.
- PHI permissions must still apply if an Admin impersonating an Operator reaches any of the print form URLs via direct navigation or API call (bypassing the UI).
Technical details:
- nothing to do except Testing

  - With no PHI permission, first_name and last_name are masked as *****.

WEB-32787: [Admin] [Support Tool] Edit PHI data on Manage Cases page with WRITE permission
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-32787
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 20; CMS Sprint 2025 21; CMS Sprint 2025 22
================================================================================

Scope
- When an Admin has the DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE permission, PHI fields on the /cases (Manage Cases) page must allow updates and save changes to the PHI Data Service.
Acceptance
- When Admin with DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE opens the Manage Cases page, PHI fields (first_name, last_name, birth_date, gender) are editable.
- The following actions trigger an update to the PHI Data Service:
  - Clicking Rename updates first_name / last_name.
  - Clicking Update birth date updates birth_date.
  - Clicking Update gender updates gender.
- Updates succeed only if PHI Data Service is available.
- If PHI Data Service is unreachable, we should consider the migration phase:
  - If it is dual-write phase:
    - PHI data is overwritten in DTX database
    - No error message required
  -  If it is PHI only phase:
    - No PHI data is overwritten.
    - An error message is shown: “Unable to update PHI data. Please try again later.”
- Updates must immediately reflect on subsequent PHI reads for the same patient.
- First name, last name input fields, date of birth calendar selection and Gender dropdown are disabled for all Admins without Write permission. (Same way way as in Shipping Address section)
- Rename, Update birth date, and Update gender buttons are disabled for all Admins without Write permission.

PS (by Moham):
For the detailed expected behavior for DUAL_WRITE_* phases refer to this message.
Technical Notes:
- ST FE
  - to start calling PHI service respecting  FF state to update the data.
  - to disable components respecting Permissions and FF

================================================================================
WEB-33650: [Support Tool] Refactor Authentication 
Status: Open
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-33650
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 22
================================================================================

As part of the PHI integration, we added HS256 support in PHI to facilitate interoperability with Support Tool. However, the best practice for applications on the external domain (like ST) is to get authenticated by SSO using backend to backend flow.
Preconditions:
- External applications (ST, CMS, etc.) exist in their own domains.
- SSO service is available and can issue JWT tokens.
- PHI service is part of the Aligners services domain.
Goal:
Ensure PHI securely authenticates requests using SSO-issued tokens, without relying on external application logic, and support S2S authentication for services across domains.
Acceptance Criteria (AC):
- PHI does not rely on the internal logic of external applications.
- PHI only accepts tokens issued by the SSO service.
- SSO supports S2S authentication for external domain services to access Aligners services.
- S2S authentication uses JWT tokens.
- JWT format and structure are defined and agreed upon.
- Each external domain has its own dedicated signing key for JWTs (e.g., separate keys for ST, CMS).
- Authentication flow is tested and verified between PHI, SSO, and at least one external service.

WEB-33107: Provide a mechanism to bypass users trigger in flyway migrations
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-33107
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 20
================================================================================

During batch updates to the users table initiated by Flyway migrations (e.g., updating access_permissions for all doctors), the table trigger generates a large number of rows in users_log.
Under normal, application-driven updates, those users_log rows are processed by a scheduled Lambda (TriggersFunction) in the regions service. However, for large updates this function is too slow, creating a bottleneck in the user sync process during go-live.
We need a way for Flyway migrations on the users table to bypass the trigger so that no users_log entries are created. After the migration completes, we will manually invoke the sync functions.

================================================================================
WEB-32300: Store PHI access token as an HttpOnly cookie 
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-32300
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 18
================================================================================

Currently, in the alignerweb application, the access token (required for making requests to PHI) is embedded directly in a JavaScript script as a variable named accessToken (an example).
If alignerweb is vulnerable to cross-site scripting (XSS), this approach poses a security risk. An attacker could exploit the XSS vulnerability to steal the access token and  make requests to PHI. 
We regularly scan the Aligners project using Burp Suite to detect potential vulnerabilities, and as of now, we have only one XSS related low-severity vulnerability that is planned to be mitigated as part of this ticket. Having said all the above, it's still suggested to adopt additional protective measures to handle the access token more securely.
One such approach is to store the access token in an HttpOnly cookie. Because HttpOnly cookies are inaccessible to JavaScript, even if an XSS vulnerability exists, malicious scripts cannot access the token—greatly reducing the risk of exposure.
Scope:
- To stop putting PHI access token to the model
- To create HttpOnly cookie
- To update every jsp page not to get PHI token from the model and start getting it from HttpOnly cookie
Acceptance Criteria:
- We should not see access token when we "view page source" in the pages:
  - ** to include the list of pages that use PHI token
- The value of access token should be viewed among cookies with HttpOnly ticked
Technical Notes:
- phi-service
  - to support authorization by http-only cookie
- alignerweb ( as the ticket is applicable only to JSP pages)
  - to remove request.getSession().setAttribute("accessToken", accessToken);
  - to put a token to the Cookies once user is authentication
  - token in cookies shouldn’t be expired earlier than session. 
  - to remove 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + accessToken
    and to update the code to send the httponly cookie to PHI

WEB-32452: [Doctor/Staff] Retrieve PHI data via PHI Data Service on /caseWizard page during case creation
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-32452
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 17; CMS Sprint 2025 18
================================================================================


Scope:
- to fetch PHI fields from the PHI Data Service when rendering the /caseWizard page during case creation
Scenarios:
- For new aligner case: Add Patient → Add Patient step: required fields filled in → Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)
- For un-submitted cases: case details → Edit details button → Patient Details (/caseWizard) → Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)
- For shipped aligner case: case details → Refinement order button → /secondaryOrder → Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)
- For shipped ODB case: case details → Combo order → Patient Details (/odbToAlignerOrder) → Scans, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)
- For shipped aligner case: case details → Spark on demand button → Add active aligners button → Patient Details (/caseWizard) → Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)
- For shipped aligner case: case details → Spark on demand button → Add passive aligners button → Patient Details (/caseWizard) → Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)
- For shipped aligner case: case details → Spark on demand button → Add Retainers button → Patient Details (/caseWizard) → Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)
- For shipped aligner case: case details → Spark on demand button → Add Spark Retainers button → Patient Details (/caseWizard) → Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps (/caseWizard)

Acceptance Criteria:
- When a Doctor/Staff creates the case and enters the Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps, DTX calls the PHI Data Service and retrieves PHI data:
  - first_name
  - last_name
- The following values must be displayed at the Scans, Product Order, Patient Prescription steps of /caseWizard page
  - first_name
  - last_name
Technical Notes:
- React pages pages to be updated:
  - /caseWizard
- DTX:
  - FE:
    - make a request to PHI with phi_patient_id based on FF value
    - to view patient data in FE from response
  - BE:
    - to check FF value
    - to provide phi_patient_id to FE
FeatureFlags:
| Migration Flag | We read data from PHI? | Error in PHI |
| NONE | CDC_AND_VALIDATION_MODE | ❌ |   |
| DUAL_WRITE_AND_READ_FROM_DTX_MODE | ❌ |   |
| DUAL_WRITE_AND_READ_FROM_PHI_FALLBACK_MODE | :successful: | DTX should show patient data from DTX DB |
| DUAL_WRITE_READ_ONLY_FROM_PHI_MODE, PHI_ONLY_MODE | :successful: | User should see error |

WEB-31716: Disable users activity report
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-31716
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 13
================================================================================

The current architecture of the User Activity Report (implemented as a Lambda function) is not able to handle the volume of data in our logs. Attempts to make it work within the Lambda limits were unsuccessful and led to a CloudWatch cost spike on June 23rd. To address this, we need to redesign the report's architecture under a separate ticket.
In the meantime, to avoid further unnecessary costs, we should disable the report until a more scalable solution is in place.

WEB-31387: Manage PHI Access Permissions in Support Tool
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-31387
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 12
================================================================================

To streamline PHI data governance and simplify permission management, three mutually exclusive PHI access permissions under the DTX section in the Support Tool should be introduced:
- Write - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE
  - Grants full read and write access to PHI data
  - Allows impersonation of Doctors
- Read Only - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY
  - Grants read-only access to PHI data
  - Does not allow impersonation or write operations
- Read Names Only - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY
  - Grants access to only patient first and last names
  - All other PHI data is returned as dummy values

User Scenario:
As a Super Admin,
I want to manage PHI data access permissions using three options in the Support Tool,
So that I can grant appropriate access levels to Admins.
Scope:
- Add a PHI Data Access dropdown under Support Tool -> Users -> Access Permissions -> DTX section with the following options:
  - None - none of the permissions is granted
  - Write - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_WRITE
  - Read Only - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_ONLY
  - Read Names Only - DTX_PHI_DATA_ACCESS_READ_NAMES_ONLY
Acceptance Criteria:
- When a Super Admin navigates to Support Tool → Users → Access Permissions, new PHI Data Access dropdown with permissions is visible under the DTX section
[attachment]
- When User clicks the dropdown, three permissions options are available:
  - None
  - Write
  - Read Only
  - Ready Names Only
- Only one permission can be selected active at a time.
- Once any permission selected it replaces the dropdown name and closes the menu.
- For existing Admin accounts Write permission is granted by default.
- For new Admin accounts None is selected
SelectedFeature Flag:
```
PHI Feature Flag
```
Technical Notes:
- add new permissions to enums
- migration for existing admin and doctor accounts
- to add dropdown in UI and to put chosen permissions to Update Access Permissions  button logic

WEB-31224: Integrate LaunchDarkly Feature Flags Based on JWT Tokens in PHI Service
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-31224
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 11; CMS Sprint 2025 12
================================================================================

Scope: Integrate LaunchDarkly into phi-service to fetch feature flags based on data from JWT tokens used by doctors. The feature flags should be retrieved dynamically for each doctor or clinic, based on their username or clinic data in the JWT token.
Acceptance Criteria:
- Fetch Feature Flags Based on JWT:
  - The application should extract relevant data (e.g., username or clinic) from the JWT token used by the doctor.
  - Based on this data, the application will query LaunchDarkly to retrieve the feature flags specific to the doctor or clinic.
    - Example fields from JWT: doctor_username, clinic_id.
- LaunchDarkly Integration:
  - The feature flags should be retrieved dynamically from LaunchDarkly based on the extracted JWT data (doctor or clinic information).
  - Ensure the system fetches and applies the appropriate flags (e.g., CDC_AND_VALIDATION_MODE, DUAL_WRITE_AND_READ_FROM_DTX_MODE, etc.) based on the user's role or clinic.
- Behavior Based on Flags:
  - Once flags are retrieved, the application's behavior should be adjusted according to the current migration phase, as controlled by the retrieved flags.
- Testing and Verification:
  - Ensure that the JWT token is correctly parsed to retrieve doctor or clinic information.
  - Verify that the application can correctly fetch feature flags for different users and clinics from LaunchDarkly.
  - Test the behavior of the system with different sets of flags for different JWTs to confirm the feature flag logic works as expected.
Technical Details:
- Use LaunchDarkly SDK to fetch feature flags for each user based on their JWT data.
- Implement logic to extract the relevant information (e.g., doctor_username, clinic_id) from the JWT token.
- Ensure that JWT parsing and feature flag retrieval work seamlessly in all environments.
- Implement fallback logic in case the JWT data is incomplete or the connection to LaunchDarkly fails (e.g., default feature flag values).

- User with insufficient permission to any component in the UI should see the component as a blank UI without seeing modal dialog too many times.

WEB-29686: As an Admin when I rollback case in Support Tool, I want to see the case is at hold status in DTX and CMS in Design Workflow
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-29686
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 10; CMS Sprint 2025 11
================================================================================

Business objective:
This story is created to fix the bug from the out of sync cases logged in [SASD-63867] cases are stuck at Registration Forward in CMS while they are moved forward to DTX to Accepted by Anatomy - Ormco R&D JIRA (atlassian.net)
User scenario:
As an Admin when I rollback a case in Support Tool, I want to see the case is at hold status in DTX and CMS in Design Workflow
Scope
- Implement a functionality of automatically placing hold on the case in DTX during the rollback of primary, refinement and reorder cases in Support Tool
Acceptance:
- If case is not on hold, then On Hold status should be set in DTX and CMS every time Admin rolls back a case to a previous step
- When Admin rolls back a case from NEW to SAVED, case is not put in hold in DTX
- On Hold status in DTX should be updated for all products and the following types of cases being roll-backed:
  - Primary
  - Refinement
  - Reorder
- If case has already been On Hold before the rollback, Hold Reason value shall not be overridden
- When On Hold status in DTX is being updated during the rollback, Rollback Hold should be used as a Hold Reason 
- When On Hold status in DTX is being updated during the rollback, a modal window should appear with the following contents:
  [attachment]- Warning message:
    'Your case is put on hold due to the rollback, please login to DTX and review the hold and remove it accordingly.' 
  - I Understand (checkbox/required)
- If User clicks ‘Rollback’ before checking ‘I Understand’ checkbox, the following error message should appear:
  'Please confirm the rollback and hold'
- When User entered a note and checked the ‘I understand’ checkbox and clicks ‘Rollback’, modal window should be closed and case should be rolled-back and automatically put on hold in DTX/CMS
Feature Flag:
This feature should be implemented under a new feature flag.
Technical Notes:
- FE change alignerweb-support
- repeat Put OnHold logic from DTX in alignerweb-suuport 
- CMS set on hold the case once StatusRollback sqs message received
  - From technical stand-point it’s critical to put case on hold in DTX before change of the status, because some auto-flows/jobs can start running before on hold status is set
- 
- DTX-CMS status map:
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/CMS/pages/4099309590/Status+map+between+CMS+and+DTX 
- Sqs message contract
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/wiki/x/IAD9WQ  
Regression Notes:
- When Admin rolls back a case in Support Tool, On Hold status in CMS external hold should be updated to ‘True’ by sent SQS message from DTX
- When Admin rolls back a case in Production Workflow via Support Tool, case is marked as ‘Inactive’ in CMS until it is released as ‘Active’ in DTX
- Because the hold is placed from DTX, therefore the case shall always be in sync status in both systems: DTX and CMS
- The rollback status shall be reflected in the process log  in CMS 
  [attachment]
- When On Hold status in DTX is being updated during the rollback, a modal window should appear with the following contents:
  - Title: Rollback status to <NEW_STATUS>
  - Notes (text field/required)
  - Notify Operator (checkbox/optional)
  - Name and Username of the User (field)
  - Rollback button
  - Close button
- When User sees the modal window, to proceed User should enter a text note (at least 1 character should be added)
- If User clicks ‘Rollback’ without adding a text, the following error message should appear:
  'Please enter notes'
- When User clicks ‘Close’ button the process should be discarded

BL-608: Implement Spring Security integration for phi-service
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/BL-608
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 09; CMS Sprint 2025 10
================================================================================

Scope:
Integrate Spring Security into phi-service using JWT-based authentication. The service must be secured following best practices and aligned with the security model used across the platform. The authentication should rely on tokens issued by the alignerweb-sso service.
Acceptance Criteria:
- phi-service is secured with Spring Security and requires valid JWTs for accessing protected endpoints.
- Tokens are validated locally without external calls for each request.
- Integration with alignerweb-sso for retrieving and validating tokens is implemented.
- Requests with invalid or expired tokens are rejected with appropriate HTTP status codes (401/403).
- Unit tests are written for the security configuration and token validation logic.
- All dependencies use the latest stable version of Spring Security.
- The implementation follows security best practices, including stateless sessions and proper exception handling.
- Documentation is updated to describe how security works in phi-service.
Technical Details:
- Use Spring Security (latest stable release).
- JWT tokens must be issued by the alignerweb-sso service and validated locally in phi-service.
- Public key or JWKS endpoint must be used to verify token signatures.
- Configure security filters to allow only authenticated access to protected endpoints.
- Avoid session-based authentication – rely solely on stateless JWT validation.
- Token claims (e.g., roles, scopes) must be extracted and mapped to Spring Security authorities.
- Create a reusable JwtAuthenticationFilter and configuration class.
- Use exception handlers to return proper error responses on authentication/authorization failures.
- Follow project structure and coding conventions for maintainability.

CMS-10213: All prioritized autoassign requests will be deprioritized after first autoassign attempt
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-10213
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 10
================================================================================

Job Story:
When an operator's auto-assign request is processed with the highest priority, but there are no available cases matching that operator’s criteria, the system should automatically lower the priority of that request. In addition to deprioritizing it, the system should delay the operator’s next assignment attempt to give other operators a chance to receive cases.
Scope:
Update the request queue priority logic after an unsuccessful first assignment attempt.
Acceptance Criteria:
- If an operator who does not have any case in their queue is trying to be auto-assigned with the new case and there is no case matching the request after the first attempt - the request should be deprioritized.
- When AC1 executed, the operator’s request should be placed at the very end of the queue before next attempt of auto-assignment.

Technical Notes:
- I operator’s request with priority = 1 has no matching cases after the first auto-assign attempt:
  - Set priority to 0
  - Update datetimestamp to CURRENT() + 10 minutes
