WEB-30183: Provide logout option when access Is denied due to recently granted permissions
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-30183
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 06; CMS Sprint 2025 07
================================================================================

As an Admin, when I try to log in to DTX immediately after being granted DTX_ACCESS permission and receive a 403 Forbidden error,
I want to have a clear option to log out and return to the login screen, 
So that I can re-authenticate and access DTX without confusion or delay.
Scope:
Implement a user-friendly logout button on the 403 Forbidden error page, allowing the user to:
- Terminate the current session.
- Be redirected to the login page for re-authentication.
Acceptance criteria (guideline):
- A Logout button shall be displayed on the error page.
- When the user clicks this button:
  - The current session is invalidated.
  - The user is redirected to the login page.
- After logout, a new session shall be created only upon successful login.
- This logout mechanism shall only be visible on 403 Forbidden pages where the lack of access is due to permission changes. For all other cases, the current error handling shall remain unchanged.
Mock-ups: Link to Figma
Feature Flag and location (desktop/web):
```
Feature Flag name
```

WEB-29562: Admin access by countries
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-29562
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 06
================================================================================

Business Value and Goal:
The current Admin role is widely assigned to employees across multiple markets. Many of the employees do not require access outside of the scope of their market area. This initiative aims to identify the smallest practical area and limit the assignment of the Admin role to only essential personnel of that area. Reducing access to Admin users based on location will enhance security and ensure employees have permissions aligned with their job requirements.
Solution:
- Find out whether we need to divide the access into smaller areas than countries (clinics). Ask the sales team. 
- Find out what regulative super-areas (regions) we need include in the UI to streamline the access management assignation.
- Define process of assigning regional data access to the Admins.
  - Manually acquire Admin user names and set of regions they need access to
- Application of the new rules: Ensure access areas align with job functions, providing employees with the data they need without granting unnecessary privileges.
Scope:
- Admin user management in Support tool
  - extend UI section Users/Access_permission  in Support tool by list of areas
- DTX data quarry 
  - Patients
  - Practices
    - Creation of practices should be also limited by region
  - Limit access to Case details

CMS-9068: Report the lead time of Spark Retainer cases that have gone through Design QC step
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9068
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 06
================================================================================

Motivation:
To identify the lead time of each Spark Retainer case that has been forwarded and accepted by Design QC operator
Scope:
Calculate the lead time data when a Spark Retainer case is forwarded and accepted by Design QC
Acceptance:
- The counting time shall start at‘Design QC Accept’ and stop at 'Pending Approval Forward'
- For all new cases = number of minutes from calculation in AC1 - minutes on external hold - weekends
- The fields in the report shall include all Spark Retainer case with the following fields:
  - Case ID
  - Order indicator
  - Lead time
  - Design QC Accept First starting date field in the format of 2024-11-05 10:45:44
  - Pending Approval Forward  Last starting date field in the format of 2024-11-05 10:45:44
- The report is titled as: spark-retainer-cases-QC-Leadtime-year-month-day
- Report will be scheduled on a daily basis
- Recipients can be added at anytime by complying the current report update process @Zhang, Ruilin 
- A copy of the report shall be placed in Share Point report folder by date
Notes:
- Report shall be ready the second day post go-live

Technical Notes:
- we store order hold history in OrderHordLog table. 
- sql script will be not just easy. MySQL or python should calculate holdminutes and exclude weekends minutes.

CMS-9981: Add roles to the "authorities" payload in JWT token
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9981
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 06
================================================================================

In our current security config in CMS, we have the user role as the custom:userRole property in JWT and authorities contains permissions only. Although we add role to authorities and effectively we are able to use @PreAuthorize to check roles as well (e.g.@PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('ROLE_USER_ADMIN')"), it is better to add roles as part of authorities in JWT token to provide more clarify especially before starting tasks of epic https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9740.

AC.  
- add roles as part of authorities in JWT token
Example:
- before:
```
{
  "sub": "admin",
  "email_verified": false,
  "custom:userRole": "ROLE_USER_ADMIN",
  "name": "Aligner",
  "custom:workflowTypes": [],
  "cognito:username": "admin",
  "exp": 1741584737,
  "iat": 1741581137,
  "family_name": "Supportas",
  "email": "dummy-email@ormco.com",
  "authorities": [
    "CMS_ACCESS"
  ]
}
```
- after
```
{
  "sub": "admin",
  "email_verified": false,
  "custom:userRole": "ROLE_USER_ADMIN",
  "name": "Aligner",
  "custom:workflowTypes": [],
  "cognito:username": "admin",
  "exp": 1741584737,
  "iat": 1741581137,
  "family_name": "Supportas",
  "email": "dummy-email@ormco.com",
  "authorities": [
    "ROLE_USER_ADMIN",
    "CMS_ACCESS"
  ]
}

```

WEB-27297: Spark Retainer: Design Automation
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-27297
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2024 26
================================================================================

Business Value and Goal:
Paint point:
- Retainer cases are passive aligners requires minimum to no design, but currently because of the lack of software automation, each retainer/passive cases requires the design technicians X amount of time to move them through design workflow which creates high labor cost
- The Design step for the Spark Retainer cases currently requires the manual intervention can be replaced by software automation
- Business predicts a high volume monthly orders for Spark Retainer following its deployment in October and made available to the customers
- DesignOps team will not be able to support such high volume of orders at some point due to the high labor cost
- software automation provides a more scalable solution for handling large volumes
Solution:
- To develop software solution and to manage the Spark retainer design workflow with automation
- Free the DesignOps team from their manual intervention in the Design workflow
Confluence page:
Design automation for Spark Retainer - Spark Web - Confluence (atlassian.net)
WSB (CMS & DTX):
- Disable autoassignment for Design  step for Spark Retainers without Doctor Notes
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-8970 
- Assigning Spark Retainers cases without Doctor Notes to Design Automation
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-27320 
- Allow Design Automation to update oas file and move cases Spark Retainers without Doctor Notes to Design QC step
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-27356
- Allow Design Automation to rollback case to COMPLETED_BY_ANATOMY in case Design Console fails to upload oas file
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/WEB-27529 
- To only allow designated QC operator to be forwarded with Spark Retainer cases without doctors notes
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9118 
- To create Design operators pool for Spark Retainer cases
- https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9127 
- Design Automation Report
  
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9069 
  https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9068 
- Auto-assign logic update for Spark Retainer cases With doctors notes
https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9257

CMS-7967: As a Design Workflow Operator I want to be prevented from forwarding case that has a user alert with 'Forward Allowed' set to 'No'
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-7967
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2024 25; CMS Sprint 2024 26
================================================================================

User Scenario:
As a Design Workflow Operator I want to be prevented from forwarding case that has a user alert with 'Forward Allowed' set to 'No'
Scope:
- Do not allow operators to forward cases if case has user alert with ‘Forward Allowed' field set to 'No’
Acceptance:
- GIVEN
  Admin enters Support → Configuration page and sets Forward Allowed field  to No
  Operator adds oas file to a case in Designer app
  WHEN User Alert is added to a case
  THEN Operator enters Assigned Case List and Forward button is disabled
- GIVEN Admin enters Support → Configuration page and sets Forward Allowed field  to No
  Operator adds oas file to a case in Designer app
  WHEN User Alert is added to a case
  THEN  Operator enters Design Details Page andForward button is disabled
- When Operator enters Case Details page and hovers over the disabled Forward button, Operator shall see the following tool tip:
  Case cannot be forwarded with this type of alert
- When Operator enters Assigned Case List page and hovers over the disabled Forward button, Operator shall see the following tool tip:
  Case cannot be forwarded with this type of alert
- GIVEN
  - Operator add oas file to a case with user alert with Forward Allowed field set to No Yes
  - Operator enters Assigned Case List
  - Admin enters Support → Configuration page and sets Forward Allowed field  to No
  - User Alert is added to a case
  WHEN
  Operator clicks Approve & Forward button in the modal window
  THEN
  Operator should see a system message:
  Case has not been forwarded, as restricting user alert has been set
Technical Notes:
NA
Regression Testing Notes:
NA
Feature Flags:
- Feature should be developed under epic feature flag

CMS-9228: To organize cases in general queue based on the existing auto-assign logic 
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9228
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2025 04
================================================================================

Job story: 
The system shall be triggered to organize cases in general queue based on the action of clicking auto-assign button by operators
Scope:
To implement a trigger to allow system assign cases to the general queue based on the existing auto-assign logic
Acceptance:
- When operator clicks the auto-assign button, the system shall be triggered to book the request and reserve it in a line of requests and pop-up shall be displayed with the following content:
  - Message: “Your requests have been received and your cases will be assigned in the order of the requests that are received among all operators accounts”
  - Ok button
  - when operator click Ok button the pop-up shall disappear (click away should close the pop-up, as it is in the current implementation)
    
    [attachment]
  - If the User has requested the new case to be assigned and the request wasn’t prioritized, and later User has 0 assigned cases in the queue and clicks auto-assign button again - the first request shall be prioritized in the request line
  - If the User has requested the new case to be assigned and the request wasn’t prioritized, and later User has >1 assigned cases in the queue and clicks auto-assign button again the same pop-up from AC 1a shall re-appear.
- The system shall look through the line of requests and assign relevant case to their corresponding operators queue
- if number of assigned cases in the operator queue =0, the system shall put that request on the top of the requests line.
- If there is no case to be assigned to the Operator, the request shall be performed during 1 hour.
- If there is no case to be assigned to the Operator in 1 hour, the System shall drop the request from the line.
Technical notes:
- To set up the frequency of processing the request line on dev environment to be able to test the order of request line processing. 
  - let’s keep it configurable using specs ( default values should be for production)
- TO DISCUSS: how to perform autoassign requests in parallel for different process steps operators 
- To put request in a queue also when operator clicks forward button instead of trying to autoassign immediately. 
- Table for the requestsLine:
  - Table name: 
  - fields: 
    id
    operatorId
    ordering_timestamp ( still can discuss the naming)
    stepId
    priority
    created
    counter ( increase it every time  before we try to assign the case to Operator from the request)
    isActive ( default true, make it false once case is assigned)
- let’s update ordering_timestamp to Now() + 10 minutes if there is no case for operator
- It should be Scheduled task (it can have Scheduled lock for different stepId)
- let's think do we need to request from masterDB instead of replica (as it is now) under the FF

This feature should be developed under new feature flag

- To reorganize the production line based on the Facility location
- When user clicks the Facility dropdown, the list of facilities will appear: 
  [attachment]- MexiCali
  - Pomona
  - Suzhou
  - Ziyang
  - Czech
- Default display will be All lines (in an organized display)
  [attachment]
- Select one or multiple facilities item will display only the associated production lines under that facilities and the name(-s) of the facility(-ies), when user selects more multiple filters, the display will show the selected names of the facilities.
  [attachment][attachment]
- The production line selection,  will be discarded upon the facility filter is selected. i.e. all the relevant the cases will be displayed from the selected facility filter
-  'All Production line” filter shall take priority over Facility filter 
- When the filter is setup for one or multiple facilities, only cases that are assigned to those facilities will be displayed
- Consequently the step filters shall also be aligned with displayed cases based on the filtered facilities
- Upon the facility is selected from the dropdown, an X button will appear, clicks on X button will clear the filter, All lines will re-appear as the default
- This feature shall be in sync with the production configuration page.

  - to add dropdown
  - to group lines by facilities

    Or it is ok to get the whole list and to filter it on FE side
  - to add Integration tests

  - to add Integration tests

CMS-9137: To add doctor-limit-of-order property to the general LimitOfOrder logic
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9137
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2024 25
================================================================================

Job Story:
The LimtOfOrder for doctors configuration shall be respected in Design operators account page
Scope:
To enable the doctor-LimitOfOrder in Designer operators account 
[attachment]Acceptance:
- The doctor-LimitOfOrder shall be considered within the general-LimitOrOrder logic for all design operators accounts when assigning new cases 
- 
- 
- Design Operator can be assigned with the following active cases
  - Case with Doctor assigned to Current Design operator, shall respect two configurations: doctor-LimitOfOrder , general-LimitOrOrde
    - for new cases, if operator didn’t reach doctor-LimitOfOrder with new cases for this particular doctor
    - for CR cases doctor-LimitOfOrder doesn’t matter
- If all of the doctor-LimitOfOrder s are reached, but the general-LimitOrOrder is not, then the operator’s queue cannot be populated with any assigned doctor’s cases , but the operator can be assigned with the cases that respect 4d, 4e,4f
- If Not all of  thedoctor-LimitOfOrder s are reached, but the general-LimitOrOrder has already been reached, then the operator cannot be assigned with any new cases

Notes:
- For example, When operators general-LimitOrOrder  is set for 4 however their doctor-LimitOfOrder is set only for 2, only 2 of that assigned doctors cases can be populated when the operator clicks the Auto-Assign button. 
Autoassign strategy description:
All green ACs are existing function and red are just added with this story:
- The doctor-LimitOfOrder shall be considered within the general-LimitOrOrder logic for all design operators accounts when assigning new cases 
- Design Operator can be assigned with active cases
  - Not On Hold cases
    - Internal
    - External
  - Not cancelled cases
- Only available operator can be assigned to a case. Design Operator is considered as available if
  - Auto-assign is switched on
  - Limit of Order or Limit of CRs > 0
- Design Operator can be assigned with the following active cases
  - Aligner cases
  - Design cases
  - New and CR cases
    - Design Operator cannot be assigned with new case if Limit of Orders - Number of Assigned New Cases < 1
    - Design Operator cannot be assigned with CR case if Limit of CRs - Number of Assigned CR Cases < 1
  - Case without any Region, POD or Doctor assigned operator
  - Case with Region assigned to Current operator and doesn’t have POD or Doctor assigned operator for this step
  - Case with POD assigned to Current operator and doesn’t have Doctor assigned operator for this step
  - Case with Doctor assigned to Current Design operator, shall respect two configurations: doctor-LimitOfOrder , general-LimitOrOrde
    - for new cases, if operator didn’t reach doctor-LimitOfOrder with new cases for this particular doctor
    - for CR cases doctor-LimitOfOrder doesn’t matter
- If all of the doctor-LimitOfOrder s are reached, but the general-LimitOrOrder is not, then the operator’s queue cannot be populated with any assigned doctor’s cases , but the operator can be assigned with the cases that respect 4d, 4e,4f
- If Not all of  thedoctor-LimitOfOrder s are reached, but the general-LimitOrOrder has already been reached, then the operator cannot be assigned with any new cases
- When Design Operator clicks Auto-assign button, operator should be assigned with one of the cases from General Queue (new or CR) that has the highest Auto-Assign Rank
Regression testing:
All green ACs are existing function and can be covered under regression testing.
Technical Notes:
- To create new AutoAssignStrategy for Designer Operator
- To update AutoAssignStrategyDetector
- To write tests

Feature Flags:
- Feature should be developed under epic feature flag

Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2024 25
================================================================================

User Scenario:
As an Admin/Operator I want to see Doctor Alert to be  added to details page of all cases related to a doctor who has alert
Scope:
- Implement a functionality of adding Doctor Alerts to Admin Detail Page of all active cases when alert is added from doctor
- Implement a functionality of adding Doctor Alerts to Operator Detail Page of all active cases when alert is added from doctor
Acceptance:

  WHEN Admin opens Detail Page of any active related to this doctor
  THEN Admin should see Doctor Alert to be added to Details Page

  THEN page should contain the following contents:
  - Doctor Alert tag:
    - Title: Doctor Alert
    - Text: Name of a category
  - he following message should appear in a Process logs:
    MM/DD/YY HH:MM A Doctor Alert <alert_category> was added
  - Entered comment is saved to Order Notes
    - Note should start with the name of Doctor Alert category
- GIVEN Admin adds Doctor Alert on CMS Support → Doctors
  WHEN Operator opens Detail Page of any active related to this doctor
  THEN Admin should see Doctor Alert to be added to Details Page
- GIVEN Admin adds Doctor Alert to a doctor
  WHEN Operator opens a Detail Page of an active case
  THEN page should contain the following contents:
  - Doctor Alert tag:
    - Title: Doctor Alert
    - Text: Name of a category
  - the following message should appear in a Process logs:
    MM/DD/YY HH:MM A Doctor Alert <alert_category> was added
  - Entered comment is saved to Order Notes
    - Note should start with the name of Doctor Alert category
- Case may have User Alert, System Alert and Doctor Alert, in this case the order should be:
  - User Alert
  - System Alert
  - Doctor Alert
Technical Notes:
NA
Regression Testing Notes:
NA
Feature Flags:
- Feature should be developed under epic feature flag

CMS-9003: Convert users table collaction to utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci
Status: Done
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-9003
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2024 22
================================================================================

Motivation:
there is an error occurred in CMS China while users from other regions are imported.  
[ERROR] InterfaceError: Error while executing statement: Conversion from collation utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci into utf8mb3_general_ci impossible for parameter
Traceback (most recent call last):
Scope:
- to apply migration to CMS like. Please very that this script works!
- ```
  DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS R2024_4_utf8mb4;
  DELIMITER $$
  CREATE PROCEDURE R2024_4_utf8mb4()
  BEGIN
      IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                         FROM information_schema.TABLES
                         WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
                           AND TABLE_COLLATION = 'utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci'
                           AND TABLE_NAME = 'users')
      THEN
          ALTER TABLE users
          CONVERT TO CHARSET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
      END IF;    
      
      IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                     FROM information_schema.COLUMNS
                     WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
                       AND COLLATION_NAME = 'utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci'
                       AND TABLE_NAME = 'users'
                       AND COLUMN_NAME = 'username')
      THEN
          ALTER TABLE users MODIFY username VARCHAR(256) CHARSET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NOT NULL;
          ALTER TABLE users MODIFY person_first_name VARCHAR(256) CHARSET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NULL;
          ALTER TABLE users MODIFY person_last_name VARCHAR(256) CHARSET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NULL;
          ALTER TABLE users MODIFY person_middle_name VARCHAR(256) CHARSET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NULL;
          ALTER TABLE users MODIFY password_cleartext VARCHAR(256) CHARSET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NULL;
          ALTER TABLE users MODIFY email VARCHAR(256) CHARSET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NULL;
      END IF;
  END$$
  DELIMITER ;
  
  call R2024_4_utf8mb4();
  DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS R2024_4_utf8mb4;
  ```
AC:
- collation of users table is utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci

CMS-6534: Rolled back from ERP Booking state case is autoapproved after ERP response received
Status: Closed
URL: https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-6534
Mentioned in: CMS Sprint 2024 21
================================================================================

Environment:
- Release QA DTX https://aligners-release.qa.ormcodigital.com/ and CMS https://aligners-cms-release.qa.ormcodigital.com/ 
steps:
- having a case at Pending Approval state
- case is ERP enabled
- as doctor in DTX approve this case
- while case is Approved by Doctor in DTX and Pending Approval ERP Booking in CMS rollback the case in Support Tool once to Pending Approval state
- wait several minutes
expected:
- after several minutes case is still Pending Approval in DTX and CMS
actual:
- after several minutes case is Approved for Manufacturing in DTX and Approved in CMS
note:
- reproducible for Primary, Refinement, Reorder (statuses are a bit different but behavior is the same) orders
- reason seems to be the same as in https://ormcornd.atlassian.net/browse/CMS-6240
